          var dotcomNavInstance = null;
          var queue = [initDotcomNav];

          function initDotcomNav(dotcomNav) {
            dotcomNav.init({
              env: 'prod',
              clientVersion: '4.23.1',
              generationId: 'GB/en/e6bf1a36-b70d-4e96-9095-ba0e2ddd6ce9',
              timestamp: new Date(1571773993318),
              messages: messages,
              initialState: initialState,
              country: 'GB',
              language: 'en',
            });
          }
          function getQueue() {
            return queue;
          }
          function NikeDotcomNavReady(callback) {
            if (dotcomNavInstance) {
              callback(dotcomNavInstance);
            } else if (queue.indexOf(callback) === -1) {
              queue.push(callback);
            }
          }
          function checkDOM(callback) {
            if (document.readyState === "loading") {
              document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", callback);
            } else {
              callback();
            }
          }
          function drainQueue(dotcomNav, done) {
            checkDOM(function onDOMContentLoaded() {
              dotcomNavInstance = dotcomNav;
              while(queue.length) {
                var next = queue.shift();
                next(dotcomNav);
              }
              done();
            });
          }
          NikeDotcomNavReady.getQueue = getQueue;
          NikeDotcomNavReady.drainQueue = drainQueue;
          window.NikeDotcomNavReady = NikeDotcomNavReady;
        })();
      </script>
